{D153}]@##/_/rgya gar skad du/_Ar+ya sA ga ra nA ga rA dza pa ri prIts+tsha nA ma ma hA yA na sU tra/_bod skad du/_'phags pa klu'i rgyal po rgya mtshos zhus pa zhes bya ba theg pa chen po'i mdo/_bam po dang po/_sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' thams cad la phyag 'tshal 
[\[116a.2\]]lo/_/'di skad bdag gis thos pa dus gcig na/_bcom ldan 'das rgyal po'i khab na/_bya rgod kyi phung po'i ri la/_dge slong brgyad stong gi dge slong gi dge 'dun chen po dang /_byang chub sems dpa' mngon par shes pa mngon par shes pas phyogs 
[\[116a.3\]]bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams nas 'dus pa/_rnam pa'i mchog thams cad dang ldan pa/_gzungs dang mdo sde rtogs par khong du chud pa/_sems can thams cad spobs pas mgu bar byed pa/_mngon par shes pa'i ye shes ston pa la mkhas pa/_pha rol tu phyin pa thams cad kyi 
[\[116a.4\]]dam pa'i pha rol tu son pa/_byang chub sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin dang snyoms par 'jug pa dang ldan pa shes pa la mkhas pa/_sangs rgyas thams cad kyis bstod cing bsngags la snyan par brjod pa/_sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad du rdzu 'phrul gyis 'gro ba shes pa la mkhas pa/_bdud 
[\[116a.5\]]thams cad skrag par bya ba shes pa la mkhas pa/_chos thams cad ji lta ba bzhin du shes pa la mkhas pa/_sems can thams cad kyi dbang po mchog dang mchog ma yin pa shes pa la mkhas pa/_byang chub kyi phyogs kyi chos thams cad yang dag par sgrub pa shes pa la mkhas pa/_
[\[116a.6\]]sangs rgyas thams cad kyi mchod pa'i las yang dag par sgrub pa shes pa la mkhas pa/_'jig rten gyi chos thams cad kyis ma gos pa/_lus dang ngag dang sems kyi rgyan thams cad kyis brgyan pa/_byams pa chen po dang /_snying rje chen po la spro ba'i go cha bgos pa/_bskal 
[\[116a.7\]]pa grangs med par brtson 'grus chen pos yid yongs su mi skyo ba/_seng ge'i sgra chen po bsgrags pa/_phas kyi rgol ba thams cad kyi zil gyis mi non pa/_phyir mi ldog pa'i chos kyi phyag rgyas btab pa/_sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad kyis dbang bskur ba thob pa khri nyis stong

[\[116b.1\]]la 'di lta ste/_byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po lhun po dang /_ri rab dang /_lhun po chen po dang /_lhun po'i rtse 'dzin dang /_lhun po mar me'i rgyal po dang /_lhun po brtsegs pa dang /_lhun po'i rgyal mtshan dang /_lhun po'i rgyal po dang /_lhun po'i rtse mo rdob pa'i

[\[116b.3\]]ba dang /_rin chen bkod pa dang /_rin chen 'od dang /_rin chen gling dang /_rin chen sgron ma dang /_lag na rin chen dang /_dga' byed dang /_chos la dga' ba bkod pa dang /_bkod pa'i rgyal po dang /_mtshan gyis yang dag par brgyan pa dang /_tshig bkod pa'i 'od dang /_tshig rnam 
[\[116b.4\]]par dag pa'i 'od dang /_gtsug na rin po che dang /_lha brtsegs dang /_rin chen brtsegs dang /_'od zer stong ldan dang /_me lce dang /_skar ma la dga' ba dang /_zla 'od dang /_sems bskyed ma thag tu chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba dang /_gser [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]rnam dag 'od dang /_byang 
[\[116b.5\]]chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rtag tu rgyun mi 'chad par mi 'jigs pa sbyin pa dang /_byams pa la sogs pa bskal pa bzang po'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po thams cad dang /_'jam dpal la sogs pa sems dpa' dpe med pa drug cu dang /_bzang skyong 
[\[116b.6\]]la sogs pa skyes bu dam pa bcu drug dang /_rgyal po chen po bzhi la sogs pa rgyal chen bzhi'i ris kyi lha rnams dang /_lha'i dbang po brgya byin la sogs pa sum cu rtsa gsum pa'i lha rnams dang /_lha'i rgyal po rab 'thab bral la sogs pa 'thab bral ba'i lha rnams dang /_
[\[116b.7\]]lha'i rgyal po yongs su dga' ldan la sogs pa dga' ldan gyi ris kyi lha rnams dang /_lha'i rgyal po rab 'phrul dga' la sogs pa 'phrul dga'i lha rnams dang /_lha'i rgyal po dbang byed la sogs pa gzhan 'phrul dbang byed kyi lha rnams dang /_lha'i bu ded dpon la sogs pa bdud 
[\[117a\]]
[\[117a.1\]]kyi ris kyi lha'i bu dkar po'i phyogs thams cad dang /_rab tshangs pa la sogs pa tshangs pa'i mdun na 'don pa'i lha rnams dang /_mi mjed kyi bdag po tshangs pa la sogs pa tshangs chen gyi ris kyi lha rnams dang /_lha'i rgyal po 'od gsal la sogs pa 'od 
[\[117a.2\]]gsal gyi lha rnams dang /_lha'i rgyal po dge rgyas la sogs pa dge rgyas kyi lha rnams dang /_lha'i rgyal po 'bras bu che la sogs pa 'bras bu che ba'i lha rnams dang /_lha'i rgyal po dbang phyug chen po la sogs pa gnas gtsang ma'i ris kyi lha'i bu rnams dang /_lha'i rgyal po 
[\[117a.3\]]dri med 'od la sogs pa 'og min gyi lha'i bu rnams dang /_lha ma yin gyi dbang po sgra gcan la sogs pa lha ma yin gyi dbang po drug khri dang /_mi'am ci'i rgyal po ljon pa la sogs pa mi'am ci'i rgyal po bzhi khri nyis stong dang /_dri za'i rgyal po phreng thogs la sogs pa dri za'i rgyal 
[\[117a.4\]]po sum khri nyis stong dang /_klu'i rgyal po ma dros pa la sogs pa klu'i rgyal po bdun khri nyis stong dang /_bya'i rgyal po nam mkha' lding bzhi stong dang /_de ma yin pa'i lha dang /_klu dang /_gnod sbyin dang /_dri za dang /_lha ma yin dang /_nam mkha' lding dang /_mi'am ci dang /_lto 
[\[117a.5\]]'phye chen po gzi brjid che ba gzi brjid che ba stong dag dang thabs cig tu bzhugs so/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das 'khor stong phrag du mas yongs su bskor cing mdun gyis bltas te chos ston cing /_lha thams cad kyi bkod pas legs par brgyan pa'i seng ge'i khri la ri'i rgyal po ri rab 
[\[117a.6\]]rgya mtsho'i nang nas gyen du 'phags pa bzhin du bzhugs te/_bcom ldan 'das kyis lha dang bcas pa'i 'jig rten zil gyis mnan nas dge slong gi tshogs kyis yongs su bskor te lham me lhan ne lhang nge zhing bzhugs so/_/de nas de ltar 'dus pa'i 'khor de'i nang na de bzhin gshegs 
[\[117a.7\]]pa de ltar bzhugs pa'i steng gi bar snang la rin po che'i gdugs rin po che thams cad kyi 'od dang ldan zhing rin po che thams cad kyis legs par brgyan pa zhig byung ste/_rin po che'i gdugs des thams cad dang ldan pa'i gling bzhi pa chen po'i 'jig rten 'di kun tu khebs par gyur to/_/
[\[117b\]]
[\[117b.1\]]rin po che'i gdugs de las kyang mu tig kha dog du ma'i rgyan phreng 'bum phrag du ma 'di lta ste/_mu tig dkar po dang /_mu tig dmar po dang /_mu tig dri ma med pa dang /_gser lta bu'i mu tig dang /_zla ba lta bu'i mu tig dang /_pad ma lta bu'i mu tig dang /_nam mkha' lta bu'i 
[\[117b.2\]]mdog can gyi mu tig rnams 'phyang ngo /_/mu tig gi rgyan phreng de dag las kyang 'od zer dag byung ngo /_/'od zer de dag las kyang me tog kha dog sna tshogs pa/_kha dog 'bum phrag du ma dri zhim pa/_blta na sdug pa yid du 'ong ba dag gi char mngon par bab bo/_/me tog de dag gis

[\[117b.4\]]ldan pa maud gal gyi bu chen po stan las langs nas bla gos phrag pa gcig tu gzar te pus mo g.yas pa'i lha nga sa la btsugs nas/_bcom ldan 'das ga la ba de logs su thal mo sbyar ba btud de/_bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to/_/bcom ldan 'das sngon ma mthong

[\[130a.1\]]chos rnams lhag par mthong bar byed/_sems can rnams yongs su smin par byed do/_/klu'i bdag po de la ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' shes rab kyi gnas la gnas nas sbyin pa sbyin par byed ce na/_klu'i bdag po 'di la sbyin pa mnyam pa nyid kyis 
[\[130a.2\]]bdag mnyam pa nyid do/_/bdag mnyam pa nyid kyis sems can mnyam pa nyid do/_/sems can mnyam pa nyid kyis chos mnyam pa nyid do/_/chos mnyam pa nyid kyis byang chub mnyam pa nyid de/_mnyam pa nyid 'di dag kyang mi spong la sbyin pa yang sbyin par byed de/_sbyin pa de 
[\[130a.3\]]yang nyon mongs pa'i dbang gis sbyin par mi byed do/_/des de ji lta ji ltar yongs su 'jal ba de kho na bzhin du nyon mongs pa thams cad yongs su gtong bar byed de/_nyon mongs pa thams cad yongs su gtong ba dang /_lta ba'i rnam pa thams cad nye bar zhi ba dang /_rtsod 
[\[130a.4\]]pa thams cad 'dor ba gang yin pa de ni gtong ba'i mchog yin te/_klu'i bdag po 'di ni byang chub sems dpa' shes rab kyi gnas la gnas pa'i sbyin pa yongs su gtong ba'o/_/de la byang chub sems dpa' shes rab kyi gnas la gnas pa'i tshul khrims gang zhe na/_
[\[130a.5\]]lus dang ngag dang sems dben par mthong ba gang yin pa de ni tshul khrims yongs su srung ba ste/_lus dang ngag dang sems la yang mi rten/_'jig rten 'di la yang mi rten/_'jig rten pha rol la yang mi rten/_nang la yang mi rten/_phyi rol la yang mi rten/_phung 
[\[130a.6\]]po la yang mi rten/_khams la yang mi rten/_skye mched la yang mi rten/_byang chub la yang mi rten/_mya ngan las 'das pa la yang mi rten/_chos thams cad la yang mi rten par tshul khrims srung bar byed la/_tshul khrims des kyang rlom sems su mi byed cing shin 
[\[130a.7\]]tu rlom sems su mi byed pa 'di ni/_byang chub sems dpa' shes rab kyi gnas la gnas pa'i tshul khrims zhes bya'o/_/de la byang chub sems dpa' shes rab kyi gnas la gnas pa'i bzod pa gang zhe na/_bzod pa gang sgom pa na bdag dang sems can dang /_srog 
[\[130b\]]
[\[130b.1\]]dang gang zag tu mi dmigs shing /_ngar 'dzin pa dang /_nga yir 'dzin pa la yang mi gnas pa de ni nang dag pa dang /_sems can dag pa dang /_chos thams cad dag pas bzod pa sgom pa yin te/_de ni bzod pa yang sgom la/_chos gang yang sgom par mi byed/_mi 
[\[130b.2\]]sgom par yang mi byed la/_chos gang yang bskyed pa'i phyir ram dgag par bya ba'i phyir yang sgom par mi byed/_chos gang yang zad par bya ba'i phyir ram/_nye bar zhi bar bya ba'i phyir yang sgom par mi byed/_sems can bdag med pa dang /_sems can dben pas kyang 
[\[130b.3\]]mi skrag mi dngang dngang bar mi 'gyur la bzod pa de yang lus la dmigs pa dang /_ngag la dmigs pa dang /_sems la dmigs pas bzod par mi byed pa dang /_de lus bshig pa'am/_yan lag dang /_nying lag rnams gcod pa na rtswa dang /_shing dang 
[\[130b.4\]]rtsig pa dang 'dra bar so sor rtog cing bzod pa dang /_de gzhan gyis ngan du smras pa thos na ngag ya ma brla dang ngag gsob dang ngag gsog dang /_ngag gzung du med pa dang /_ngag zhi ba dang /_ngag mi gnas par shes nas bzod pa dang /_de sems rnyog pa med pa 
[\[130b.5\]]dang /_sems gsar ma yin pa dang /_sems rnying ma yin pa dang /_gcig la gcig mtshams sbyor ba med pa dang /_skad cig gis 'jig pas med par 'gyur bar so sor rtog cing bzod la/_mtshams sbyor ba med par mthong ba 'di ni byang chub sems dpa' shes 
[\[130b.6\]]rab kyi gnas la gnas pa'i bzod pa zhes bya'o/_/de la byang chub sems dpa' shes rab kyi gnas la gnas pa'i brtson 'grus gang zhe na/_byang chub sems dpa' dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad rnam par 'phel bar bya ba'i phyir brtson 'grus kyang rtsom la/_brtson 'grus rnam 
[\[130b.7\]]par 'phel ba des kyang chos kyi dbyings rnam par 'phel ba dang yongs su 'grib pa yang dag par rjes su mi mthong ba gang yin pa dang /_des chos thams cad chos kyi dbyings su yang dag par 'du bar rig nas chos gang yang bden par yongs su grub par yang dag par rjes su mi 
[\[131a\]]
[\[131a.1\]]mthong la/_mi bden pa phyin ci log la 'jig rten gnas pa yang mthong ba dang /_des de ltar shes rab rnam par dag pa'i sgo nas chos thams cad la rnam par lta ba na chos rnams 'phel bar yang mi byed/_'grib pa yang mi byed pa dang /_chos 
[\[131a.2\]]thams cad spungs pa'i rang bzhin ma yin pa dang /_yul na mi gnas pa dang /_phyogs na mi gnas pa dang /_gang nas kyang mi 'ong ba dang /_gang du yang mi 'gro bar rab tu shes te/_de de lta bu'i chos dang ldan pas phyin ci log pa yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhin du yongs 
[\[131a.3\]]su shes par bya ba'i phyir/_sems can rnams la chos ston pa'i brtson 'grus rtsom la/_sems can de dag kyang yang dag par mi dmigs te/_sems can mi dmigs pa gang yin pa de nyid de'i chos thams cad mi dmigs pa'o/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_sems can la 
[\[131a.4\]]ma gtogs pa'i chos gang yang med pa la/_chos la ma gtogs pa'i sems can rnams kyang med mod kyi/_'on kyang chos kyi ngo bo nyid gang yin pa de ni bdag gi ngo bo nyid do/_/bdag gi ngo bo nyid gang yin pa de ni chos thams cad kyi ngo bo nyid do/_/chos 
[\[131a.5\]]thams cad kyi ngo bo nyid gang yin pa de ni sangs rgyas kyi chos rnams kyi ngo bo nyid do/_/ngo bo nyid mnyam pa nyid 'dis sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad tshol ba gang yin pa dang /_btsal nas kyang chos kyang mi dmigs/_chos ma yin pa yang mi dmigs pa dang /_
[\[131a.6\]]tshol ba na yang gang gis btsal ba dang /_gang btsal ba dang /_sus btsal ba de dag thams cad mi dmigs pa 'di ni byang chub sems dpa' shes rab kyi gnas la gnas pa'i brtson 'grus zhes bya'o/_/de la byang chub sems dpa' shes rab kyi gnas la gnas pa'i 
[\[131a.7\]]bsam gtan gang zhe na/_bsam gtan rnams la yang snyoms par 'jug la/_mnyam pa nyid las kyang nyams par yang mi 'gyur/_phun sum tshogs par yang mi 'gyur ba gang yin pa dang /_de bsam gtan kyang bsam gtan du byed la/_chos gang yang 
[\[131b\]]
[\[131b.1\]]skyed par yang mi byed/_'bri bar yang mi byed pa dang /_yul thams cad la yang mi rten par bsam gtan byed la/_chos thams cad dang 'thun pa dang mi 'thun pa med par yang bsam gtan byed pa dang /_lus dang sems kyis kyang bsam gtan gyi yan lag 
[\[131b.2\]]dang /_bsam gtan gyi bsam pa dang /_bsam gtan gyi sbyor ba de dag mngon par sgrub par mi byed pa dang /_de snyoms par 'jug pa na ci nas de bzhin nyid dang /_yang dag pa'i mtha' dang /_chos kyi dbyings kyis snyoms par 'jug pa de lta bus snyoms par 'jug 
[\[131b.3\]]pa dang /_de sems can thams cad mnyam pa nyid dang /_chos thams cad la sgro mi 'dogs pas snyoms par 'jug pa ste/_de de ltar ting nge 'dzin sgom pa'i sbyor ba la brtson pa na/_nang dang phyi rol gyi lus dang sems la yang mi gnas la/_phyi rol gyi yul la 
[\[131b.4\]]yang sems mi gnas pa de ni mi gnas pa'i rnam par shes pa ste/_dmigs pa can gyi rang bzhin gyi snyoms par 'jug pa thams cad kyang zil gyis gnon la/_phyi rol gyi drang srong mngon par shes pa lnga dang ldan pa rnams dang /_nyan thos dang /_rang sangs rgyas thams 
[\[131b.5\]]cad dang /_bsam gtan dang /_bsam gtan la snyoms par 'jug pa thams cad kyang zil gyis gnon to/_/de dge ba'i rtsa ba rnams dang /_bsam gtan de dag kyang shes rab kyi shas che ba dang /_lta ba'i nyon mongs pa dang bral ba yin la/_byang chub tu yongs 
[\[131b.6\]]su bsngos pa yang yin pa dang /_sems can yongs su smin par byed pa yang sgrub pa yin te/_de lta bas na de bzhin gshegs pa ni snyoms par 'jug pa gcig gis yongs su mya ngan las 'da' ba'i mtha'i bar du thug par mdzad de/_'di ni byang chub sems dpa' 
[\[131b.7\]]shes rab kyi gnas la gnas pa'i bsam gtan no/_/de la byang chub sems dpa' shes rab kyi gnas la gnas pa'i chos lhag par mthong ba gang zhe na/_des chos gang lhag par mthong ba de dag shes rab kyi mig kho nas lhag par mthong gi/_sha'i mig dang /_lha'i 
[\[132a\]]
[\[132a.1\]]mig gis chos de dag lhag par mthong ba ma yin no/_/de dag zhi ba kho nar lhag par mthong gi ma zhi bar lhag par mthong ba ma yin no/_/de bzhin du chos rnams rgyu ba med pa dang /_gnas pa med pa dang /_dben pa dang /_yongs su grub pa med 
[\[132a.2\]]par lhag par mthong ngo /_/de ltar mthong ba gang yin pa de ni chos thams cad lhag par mthong ba'o/_/des chos thams cad lhag par mthong ba ni/_gal te chos 'ga' zhig mthong na ni lhag mthong ma yin te/_de ci'i phyir zhe na/_chos mthong bas ni lhag mthong skyes pa ma 
[\[132a.3\]]yin la/_shes pa ni mi shes pa yin pa'i phyir ro/_/ma mthong ba ni chos lhag par mthong ba yin la/_bdag ma mthong ba dang /_sems can ma mthong ba dang /_srog dang /_gso ba dang /_skyes bu dang /_gang zag ma mthong ba ni chos lhag par mthong ba yin te/_
[\[132a.4\]]de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' ni chos de dag kyang ji lta ba bzhin du mthong la/_sems can rnams kyang phyin ci log tu gyur par mthong nas/_de'i tshe byang chub sems dpa' de kye ma chos 'di dag ni 'di ltar dag na sems can 'di dag ni/_kun nas nyon mongs so snyam nas 
[\[132a.5\]]sems can rnams la ches brten par snying rje chen po skye'o/_/de bas na de sems can rnams rab tu grol bar bya ba'i phyir/_snying brtse ba chen po skyed do/_/sems can de dag kyang rtag tu sems can med pa kho na ste/_'di ni byang chub sems dpa' shes rab kyi gnas la 
[\[132a.6\]]gnas pa'i chos lhag par mthong ba'o/_/de la byang chub sems dpa' shes rab kyi gnas la gnas pa'i sems can yongs su smin par bya ba gang zhe na/_klu'i bdag po 'di la byang chub sems dpa' ni sems can med pa'i phyir/_sems can rnams yongs su smin par byed do/_/
[\[132a.7\]]sems can bdag med pa dang /_sems can la sems can med pa dang /_sems can dben pa dang /_sems can rang bzhin gyis sems can du brjod pa tsam du zad pa dang /_sems can du gdags pa tsam du zad pa dang /_sems can stong pa dang /_sems can mtshan 
[\[132b\]]
[\[132b.1\]]ma med pa dang /_sems can smon pa med pa dang /_sems can mngon par 'du byed pa med pa dang /_sems can ji lta ba bzhin ma yin pa dang /_sems can ma skyes pa dang /_sems can ma byung ba dang /_sems can rnam par dag pa'i phyir sems can rnams yongs su smin 
[\[132b.2\]]par byed de/_de yang bdag gzhig pa'i phyir ma yin/_sems can gzhig pa'i phyir ma yin/_srog dang /_gso ba dang /_skyes bu dang /_gang zag gzhig pa'i phyir ma yin/_dngos po gzhig pa'i phyir ma yin/_rdzas gzhig pa'i phyir ma yin gyi/_klu'i bdag po 
[\[132b.3\]]sems can de dag gi ngo bo nyid ji lta ba de kho na bzhin du yongs su smin par byed do/_/sems can rnams kyi ngo bo nyid ji lta bu zhe na/_'di lta ste/_rang bzhin gyis bdag med pa'i ngo bo nyid ni ngo bo nyid med pa'o/_/ngo bo nyid med pa ni sems can rnams kyi ngo bo nyid do/_/sems 
[\[132b.4\]]can rnams kyi ngo bo nyid gang yin pa'i ngo bo nyid de nyid ni/_chos thams cad kyi ngo bo nyid do/_/chos thams cad kyi ngo bo nyid gang yin pa de nyid ni sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad kyi ngo bo nyid de/_de bas na chos thams cad ni sangs rgyas kyi chos zhes bya'o/_/klu'i bdag po chos 
[\[132b.5\]]thams cad ces bya ba ni ming tsam du zad de/_ming de yang ming du mi dmigs so/_/klu'i bdag po chos thams cad ces bya ba bstan pa ni chos ma yin pa bstan pa yin no/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_ci tsam du chos tha dad du byed pa de tsam du chos ma yin pa'i phyir ro/_/de la chos gang brjod pa de dag thams 
[\[132b.6\]]cad ni chos ma yin pa kho na'o/_/klu'i bdag po chos nyid dang chos kyi dbyings ni rang bzhin gyis brjod pa'am smra bar mi nus te/_'di ltar chos kyi dbyings kyi rang bzhin brjod du med pa dang /_chos thams cad chos kyi dbyings kyi rang bzhin can yin te/_de lta bas na chos 
[\[132b.7\]]thams cad brjod par mi nus so/_/klu'i bdag po sangs rgyas kyi chos 'di dag ni rnam grangs 'di dang tshul 'dis bstan mod kyi/_'on kyang sangs rgyas kyi chos de dag ni sgra'i rnam par rig par byed kyis rnam par rig par bya ba ma yin no/_/klu'i bdag po sangs rgyas kyi chos

[\[133a.1\]]rnams ni 'dus byas dang /_'dus ma byas kyi chos rnams kyis rnam par rig par bya ba ma yin/_gdags par bya ba ma yin no/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_klu'i bdag po 'dus byas dang /_'dus ma byas la ma gtogs par gang gzhan du bgrang du rung ba'i chos 
[\[133a.2\]]ci yang med do/_/gsol pa/_bcom ldan 'das 'o na sangs rgyas kyi chos rnams 'dus ma bgyis pa lags sam/_de bzhin gshegs pa yang de lta bu lags sam/_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_klu'i bdag po de de bzhin no/_/de de bzhin te/_sangs 
[\[133a.3\]]rgyas kyi chos rnams kyang 'dus ma byas pa'o/_/de bzhin gshegs pa yang 'dus ma byas pa'o/_/klu'i bdag po de la ji snyam du sems/_'dus ma byas pa la gdags pa ci yang yod dam/_gsol pa/_bcom ldan 'das de ni ma lags so/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis 
[\[133a.4\]]bka' stsal pa/_klu'i bdag po rnam grangs 'dis kyang khyod kyis ji ltar sangs rgyas kyi chos rnams rnam par rig par bya ba ma yin pa dang /_gdags par bya ba ma yin pa de ltar rig par bya'o/_/sangs rgyas kyi chos rnams ji lta ba bzhin du chos thams cad kyang de dang 'dra'o/_/
[\[133a.5\]]klu'i bdag po sems can rnams la de bzhin gshegs pa'i thugs rje chen po ji tsam du bstan pa la ltos/_'di ltar rnam par rig par bya ba ma yin pa dang /_gdags par bya ba ma yin pa'i chos rnams ming dang brdas 'dogs par mdzad de/_chos 'di dag ni zag pa dang 
[\[133a.6\]]bcas pa'o/_/chos 'di dag ni zag pa med pa'o/_/de bzhin du 'jig rten pa rnams dang /_'jig rten las 'das pa rnams dang /_kha na ma tho ba dang bcas pa rnams dang /_kha na ma tho ba med pa rnams dang /_'dus byas rnams dang /_'dus ma byas rnams dang /_kun 
[\[133a.7\]]nas nyon mongs pa'i phyogs dang 'thun pa rnams dang /_rnam par byang ba'i phyogs dang 'thun pa rnams dang /_bsten par bya ba rnams dang /_yongs su spang bar bya ba rnams dang /_gdags par bya ba rnams dang /_gzhig par bya ba rnams dang /_so so'i skye bo'i

[\[133b.1\]]chos rnams dang /_slob pa'i chos rnams dang /_mi slob pa'i chos rnams dang /_rang sangs rgyas kyi chos rnams dang /_byang chub sems dpa'i chos rnams dang /_sangs rgyas kyi chos rnams 'dogs par mdzad de/_klu'i bdag po de ltar na de bzhin gshegs pa ni de ltar

[\[133b.3\]]ri mor bris te/_lha'i gzugs rnams kyang der bstan/_mi'i gzugs rnams dang /_rta dang /_glang po che dang /_shing rta dang /_dpung bu chung dang /_bzhon pa rnams kyang der bstan na/_klu'i bdag po 'di ji snyam du sems/_ci mi de dka' ba byed pa yin nam/_

[\[133b.6\]]'di ltar bstan pa'i chos 'di dag la mos par 'gyur ba'am/_'jug par 'gyur ba de dag ni ches ngo mtshar che ba byed pa yin no/_/klu'i bdag po gzhan yang zab mo'i tshul 'di la 'jug pa'i sems can gang yin pa de dag ni sngon gyi de bzhin gshegs pa rnams la yongs su sbyang ba

[\[134a.1\]]tshangs par spyod pa yang spyad na/_'on kyang de bzhin gshegs pa de dag gis nga la 'di ltar zab cing 'di ltar rgyu ba chad pa'i chos ma bstan te/_gzhan du na sbyor ba'i gtam nyi tshe dang /_sbyin pa dang /_dul ba dang /_legs par sdom pa dang /_des pa'i gtam 
[\[134a.2\]]nyi tshe dang /_tshul khrims dang /_thos pa dang /_gtong ba dang /_bzod pa dang /_brtson 'grus dang /_bag yod pa'i gtam nyi tshe dang /_tshangs par spyad pa dang /_sbyangs pa'i yon tan dang yo byad bsnyungs pa'i gtam nyi tshe bstan to/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_'di ltar 
[\[134a.3\]]yongs su sbyang ba ma byas pa'i phyir ro/_/klu'i bdag po gang gi tshe nga yongs su sbyang ba byas par gyur pa de'i tshe ni ngas de bzhin gshegs pa mar me mdzad chen po las zab mo'i chos kyi tshul 'di thos so/_/thos nas kyang nga 'thun pa'i chos rnams la bzod pa skyes so/_/klu'i bdag 
[\[134a.4\]]po de lta bas na rnam grangs 'dis kyang khyod kyis 'di ltar zab cing nga'o snyam pa'i nga rgyal las rnam par grol ba yongs su rtog pas byas pa/_rgyu dang rkyen las byung ba/_sems can dang srog dang /_gso ba dang /_gang zag tu rtog pa med pa'i chos rnams la mos pa dang /_
[\[134a.5\]]dad pa dang 'dzin pa dang /_gzhan dag la yang brda sprod par byed pa'i sems can de dag ji ltar sngon gyi de bzhin gshegs pa rnams la yongs su sbyang ba byas pa yin pa dang /_de dag la ni bsod nams kyang mang du 'phel bar 'gyur ba de ltar rig par bya'o/_/klu'i bdag po byang chub 
[\[134a.6\]]sems dpa' sems can rnams kyi phan pa dang bde ba'i don la zhugs pa/_gang gis stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams na sems can lha rnams dang /_mi rnams kyi bde ba'i yo byad ji snyed yod pa'i bde ba'i yo byad de snyed sems can re re la byin na/_klu'i bdag 
[\[134a.7\]]po 'di ji snyam du sems/_ci byang chub sems dpa' des sems can de dag la bde ba'i yo byad mang po byin pa yin nam/_gsol pa/_bcom ldan 'das mang lags so/_/bde bar gshegs pa mang lags so/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_klu'i bdag po 
[\[134b\]]
[\[134b.1\]]byang chub sems dpas sems can de dag la bde ba'i yo byad de snyed byin pa gang yin pa dang /_de dag la mi rtag pa dang ldan pa tshig gcig tsam bstan tam/_sdug bsngal ba dang ldan pa'am/_bdag med pa dang ldan pa'am/_zhi ba dang ldan pa'am/_stong pa nyid dang ldan 
[\[134b.2\]]pa'am/_mtshan ma med pa dang ldan pa'am/_smon pa med pa dang ldan pa'am/_sems can med pa dang /_srog med pa dang /_gso ba med pa dang /_gang zag med pa dang ldan pa'am/_ma skyes ma byung ba dang ldan pa tshig gcig tsam bstan na/_de nyid des bde ba'i yo byad 
[\[134b.3\]]de bas ches mang du byin pa yin te/_bsod nams kyang ches mang du 'phel bar 'gyur ro/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_klu'i bdag po 'dus byas kyi bde ba'i yo byad thams cad ni sems can rnams kyis sngon myong zin gyi 'dus ma byas kyi bde ba'i yo byad ni sems can rnams kyis sngon ma 
[\[134b.4\]]myong ba'i phyir ro/_/klu'i bdag po de la 'di ltar zab pa'i chos 'di dag thos pa gang yin pa 'di ni 'dus ma byas kyi bde ba'i yo byad 'dren pa yin te/_de bas na klu'i bdag po byang chub sems dpa' bdag gi don dang /_gzhan gyi don yongs su rdzogs par byed 'dod pa rnams 
[\[134b.5\]]kyis ni chos zab mo rnams la brtson par bya'o/_/klu'i bdag po byang chub sems dpa' 'khor gyi nang du 'ongs la/_'khor gyi nang du 'dug nas zab mo'i gtam gyi bar chad byed de/_gtam sna tshogs gleng ba gang yin pa de ni dam pa'i chos nub par bya ba'i phyir brtson pa yin 
[\[134b.6\]]na/_de ci'i phyir zhe na/_'dzam bu'i gling 'dir ji srid du zab mo'i chos grags pa de srid du dam pa'i chos nub par mi 'gyur ba'i phyir ro/_/klu'i bdag po gzhan yang mi chos 'dod de/_chos nyan pa ni nyung la mi ma yin pa'i chos nyan pa ni mang ste/_der dam pa'i chos smra

[\[153b.3\]]pa med/_/seng ge'i sgra can mi 'jigs sgrogs par byed/_/pha rol tshogs rnams thams cad rab tu 'joms/_/sems can g.yo can gdul dka' nga rgyal khengs/_/mngon pa'i nga rgyal skyon ldan gang yin pa/_/de dag mkhas pa'i chos ni thob gyur na/_/nga 
[\[153b.4\]]rgyal spangs nas rkang mthil 'dud par byed/_/chos kyi dbyings ni rang bzhin zhi ba dang /_/don rtogs dang ni chos nyid nges par shes/_/de phyir chos ni gtan la 'bebs pa dang /_/yi ge 'byed la de yi tshig mi zad/_/sems can rang bzhin rnam dag chos kyi dbyings/_/
[\[153b.5\]]de bzhin bdag nyid rnam dag de yis shes/_/de ltar sems can de bzhin nyid shes pas/_/de yi chos rnams bstan pa thogs pa med/_/zad pa nam yang zad par mi nus la/_/mi zad pa yang nams kyang zad mi 'gyur/_/chos nyid rab zhi 'di ni rtogs nas kyang /_/
[\[153b.6\]]der ni mdo sde bye ba mtha' yas ston/_/yi ge 'di dag lus la mi gnas shing /_/yid la mi gnas sems las ma byung ste/_/ji ltar brag ca'i sgra ni thos pa bzhin/_/yi ge rnams kyi rang bzhin stong zhing dben/_/gzungs 'di yi ge rnams dang sgra la med/_/tshig gi

[\[154a.1\]]rab tu 'thad pa'i chos kyang ston/_/gang tshe de yis gzungs ni thob gyur na/_/so so yang dag rig bzhi rjes su rtogs/_/don la rab bslabs chos nyid cho ga shes/_/sgra dang skad dang nges pa'i tshig la mkhas/_/de bzhin spobs pa thogs med tshad kyang

[\[183a.1\]]'das kyis rjes su bstan na legs so legs so/_/de skad ces gsol pa dang /_bcom ldan 'das kyis rgya mtsho chen po'i lha mo 'od ldan ma la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to/_/'od ldan ma chos bcu po 'di dag ni rgyan du 'gyur ba yin te/_bcu

[\[183b.1\]]rgyas kyi zhing thams cad brgyan pa'i slad du bdag cag gis bskyed lags so/_/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa bzhugs kyang rung /_yongs su mya ngan las 'das kyang rung ste/_bdag ni rgya mtsho chen po'i nang na gnas bzhin du dam pa'i chos yongs
